<message type="tool">     1	"""TASK-QAV-004 — L4 behavioural round-trip oracle guard.
     2	
     3	Deterministic backstop: when the Coach's ``behavioural_oracle`` bundle reports
     4	``ran-and-failed``, an approving Coach verdict is overridden to ``feedback``
     5	with a ``must_fix`` issue naming the oracle and its failure output.
     6	
     7	Tests model the guard verbatim on the
     8	``_reconcile_absent_independent_test_signal`` archetype (wired at the same
     9	post-verdict seam, beside the COACHFG01 and spec-gap guards).
    10	
    11	Tests exercise every outcome branch:
    12	  - ran-and-failed → hard RED override (AC-1)
    13	  - timeout → ran-and-failed (AC-6)
    14	  - failed-to-start → absent WARN (AC-6)
    15	  - None bundle → no-op (AC-3)
    16	  - None oracle field → no-op (AC-3)
    17	  - passing oracle → no override (AC-4)
    18	  - not_independent → no override, warning recorded (AC-5)
    19	  - absent → no override (AC-7)
    20	  - dogfood end-to-end with real failing oracle (AC-8)
    21	
    22	Async tests use ``asyncio.run`` to stay free of a pytest-asyncio dependency,
    23	matching the convention in ``test_coach_synthesis_split.py``.
    24	"""
    25	
    26	from __future__ import annotations
    27	
    28	import asyncio
    29	import json
    30	import textwrap
    31	from pathlib import Path
    32	from typing import Any, Dict, Optional
    33	from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock, patch
    34	
    35	import pytest
    36	import yaml
    37	
    38	from guardkit.orchestrator.agent_invoker import AgentInvoker
    39	from guardkit.orchestrator.coach_verification import HonestyVerification
    40	from guardkit.orchestrator.harness import (
    41	    AssistantMessageEvent,
    42	    ResultMessageEvent,
    43	)
    44	from guardkit.orchestrator.quality_gates.coach_evidence import (
    45	    CoachEvidenceBundle,
    46	)
    47	
    48	
    49	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    50	# helpers
    51	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52	
    53	
    54	def _make_invoker(worktree: Path) -&gt; AgentInvoker:
    55	    """A minimal AgentInvoker able to run the full ``invoke_coach`` synthesis
    56	    path (mirrors ``_make_invoker_for_routing`` in test_coach_synthesis_split)."""
    57	    invoker = AgentInvoker.__new__(AgentInvoker)
    58	    invoker.worktree_path = worktree
    59	    invoker.sdk_timeout_seconds = 600
    60	    invoker._calculate_sdk_timeout = MagicMock(return_value=600)  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    61	    invoker._venv_python = None
    62	    return invoker
    63	
    64	
    65	def _bundle(
    66	    behavioural_oracle: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    67	) -&gt; CoachEvidenceBundle:
    68	    """A minimal bundle whose behavioural_oracle leg is whatever the test supplies."""
    69	    return CoachEvidenceBundle(
    70	        honesty=HonestyVerification(
    71	            verified=True, discrepancies=[], honesty_score=1.0, resolved_paths=[]
    72	        ),
    73	        gathering_status="complete",
    74	        behavioural_oracle=behavioural_oracle,
    75	    )
    76	
    77	
    78	def _approve_events(task_id: str, turn: int) -&gt; list:
    79	    """Harness events carrying a fenced ``approve`` verdict."""
    80	    verdict: Dict[str, Any] = {
    81	        "task_id": task_id,
    82	        "turn": turn,
    83	        "decision": "approve",
    84	        "rationale": "All Player-reported gates pass; tests look green.",
    85	        "criteria_verification": [],
    86	    }
    87	    text = "```json\n" + json.dumps(verdict) + "\n```"
    88	    return [AssistantMessageEvent(text=text), ResultMessageEvent(session_id=None)]
    89	
    90	
    91	def _run_coach(
    92	    invoker: AgentInvoker,
    93	    *,
    94	    task_id: str,
    95	    turn: int,
    96	    bundle: CoachEvidenceBundle,
    97	    player_report: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    98	):
    99	    """Invoke the Coach with ``_invoke_with_role`` mocked to return the
   100	    approve-verdict harness events. Everything else runs for real."""</message>
<message type="tool">     1	"""AC-014 — CoachValidator.gather_evidence returns a populated CoachEvidenceBundle.
     2	
     3	TASK-HMIG-008R Part E (Revision 3). Verifies the deterministic gathering
     4	pipeline introduced in Part A: ``CoachValidator.gather_evidence`` runs the
     5	same helpers ``validate()`` uses (``_verify_honesty``, ``verify_quality_gates``,
     6	``validate_requirements``, ``run_independent_tests``) and packages the
     7	intermediates into a :class:`CoachEvidenceBundle` instead of applying
     8	decision logic.
     9	
    10	Falsifier shape:
    11	
    12	* A passing-turn fixture must yield ``gathering_status == "complete"`` and a
    13	  bundle with populated ``quality_gates``, ``arch_review``, ``plan_audit``,
    14	  and ``tests`` fields. ``honesty.verified`` is True. JSON-serialisation via
    15	  ``bundle.to_dict()`` works for the entire bundle.
    16	* A state-bridge-move fixture (Player report contains a path the
    17	  orchestrator moved mid-turn, canonical path resolves) must populate
    18	  ``bundle.honesty.resolved_paths`` so the LLM Coach receives the
    19	  Layer-1 audit trail.
    20	* A pre-evidence error (missing ``task_work_results.json``) must produce
    21	  ``gathering_status == "partial_exception"`` with a populated
    22	  ``gathering_error`` rather than raising to the caller. This is the
    23	  Phase 2.5-review-finding-#1 contract: ``gather_evidence`` MUST NOT
    24	  raise so the autobuild primary path never falls back to ``validate()``.
    25	
    26	These tests do not assert decision logic; that is the
    27	``_apply_legacy_decision_logic`` shim's responsibility (Part A.2 follow-up)
    28	and the LLM Coach's responsibility under the primary path.
    29	"""
    30	
    31	from __future__ import annotations
    32	
    33	import json
    34	import subprocess
    35	from pathlib import Path
    36	from unittest.mock import patch
    37	
    38	import pytest
    39	
    40	from guardkit.orchestrator.coach_verification import CoachVerifier
    41	from guardkit.orchestrator.quality_gates.coach_evidence import (
    42	    CoachEvidenceBundle,
    43	    GatheringStatus,
    44	)
    45	from guardkit.orchestrator.quality_gates.coach_validator import CoachValidator
    46	
    47	
    48	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49	# Fixture helpers
    50	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    51	
    52	
    53	def _init_git_worktree(path: Path) -&gt; None:
    54	    """Minimal git init so TaskStateBridge can construct without raising."""
    55	    subprocess.run(
    56	        ["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=path, check=True, capture_output=True
    57	    )
    58	    subprocess.run(
    59	        ["git", "-C", str(path), "config", "user.email", "t@t"], check=True, capture_output=True,
    60	    )
    61	    subprocess.run(
    62	        ["git", "-C", str(path), "config", "user.name", "t"], check=True, capture_output=True,
    63	    )
    64	
    65	
    66	def _passing_task_work_results() -&gt; dict:
    67	    """Return a task_work_results dict where every gate passes."""
    68	    return {
    69	        "task_id": "TASK-X",
    70	        "quality_gates": {
    71	            "all_passed": True,
    72	            "tests_run": 12,
    73	            "tests_failed": 0,
    74	            "coverage_met": True,
    75	            "line_coverage": 0.85,
    76	            "branch_coverage": 0.78,
    77	        },
    78	        "code_review": {"score": 80, "solid_score": 85, "dry_score": 78, "yagni_score": 82},
    79	        "plan_audit": {"status": "passed", "violations": 0, "severity": "low"},
    80	        "files_modified": [],
    81	        "files_created": [],
    82	        "tests_written": [],
    83	    }
    84	
    85	
    86	def _write_results(worktree: Path, task_id: str, results: dict) -&gt; None:
    87	    results_dir = worktree / ".guardkit" / "autobuild" / task_id
    88	    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    89	    (results_dir / "task_work_results.json").write_text(json.dumps(results))
    90	
    91	
    92	@pytest.fixture
    93	def worktree(tmp_path: Path) -&gt; Path:
    94	    """A git worktree with a default-honest passing-turn fixture written."""
    95	    _init_git_worktree(tmp_path)
    96	    _write_results(tmp_path, "TASK-X", _passing_task_work_results())
    97	    return tmp_path

<message type="tool">     1	"""Cross-repo evidence contract seam test (TASK-AB-XREPOEV01 AC-006-adjacent).
     2	
     3	CI enforcement for the namespace-hygiene / absent-signal posture: a feature
     4	that declares ``evidence_repos`` must fail **loudly** if the orchestrator
     5	stops supporting it, never be silently degraded to absent-signal (the
     6	``.claude/rules/absence-of-failure-is-not-success.md`` /
     7	``.claude/rules/namespace-hygiene.md`` meta-class; the guardkit &lt;-&gt;
     8	guardkitfactory boundary that ``harness-cancellation-contract.md`` governs).
     9	
    10	Unlike a full autobuild run, these are ``inspect.signature`` + model-field
    11	assertions that fail in seconds when any link in the wiring chain is removed:
    12	
    13	    Feature.evidence_repos (declaration)
    14	        -&gt; resolve_evidence_repos (resolution)
    15	        -&gt; AgentInvoker / CoachVerifier / CoachValidator /
    16	           WorktreeCheckpointManager / AutoBuildOrchestrator (consumers)
    17	        -&gt; CoachEvidenceBundle.evidence_repo_tests (AC-002 landing site)
    18	
    19	If a future refactor drops the ``evidence_repos`` parameter from any consumer
    20	constructor, or the field from the Feature model, this seam fails in CI
    21	instead of silently producing "0 files modified" false-reds on a live run.
    22	"""
    23	
    24	from __future__ import annotations
    25	
    26	import inspect
    27	import subprocess
    28	
    29	import pytest
    30	
    31	pytestmark = [pytest.mark.seam]
    32	
    33	
    34	def _has_param(func, name: str) -&gt; bool:
    35	    return name in inspect.signature(func).parameters
    36	
    37	
    38	def _init_git(path) -&gt; None:
    39	    """Both orchestrators validate ``repo_root`` is a git repo on construction."""
    40	    subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=path, check=True, capture_output=True)
    41	
    42	
    43	class TestEvidenceContractDeclaration:
    44	    def test_feature_model_has_evidence_repos_field(self):
    45	        from guardkit.orchestrator.feature_loader import Feature
    46	
    47	        assert "evidence_repos" in Feature.model_fields
    48	
    49	    def test_malformed_declaration_fails_loudly_not_silently(self):
    50	        # The rule's core: a typo'd / malformed evidence_repos declaration must
    51	        # raise at parse time, not be silently swallowed (absent-signal).
    52	        from pydantic import ValidationError
    53	
    54	        from guardkit.orchestrator.feature_loader import Feature
    55	
    56	        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
    57	            Feature(id="FEAT-X", name="x", evidence_repos=[123])
    58	        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
    59	            Feature(id="FEAT-X", name="x", evidence_repos=[{"no_path": "y"}])
    60	
    61	    def test_resolver_is_importable(self):
    62	        from guardkit.orchestrator.evidence_repos import resolve_evidence_repos
    63	
    64	        assert callable(resolve_evidence_repos)
    65	
    66	
    67	class TestEvidenceContractConsumers:
    68	    """Every consumer constructor must still accept ``evidence_repos``."""
    69	
    70	    def test_agent_invoker_accepts_evidence_repos(self):
    71	        from guardkit.orchestrator.agent_invoker import AgentInvoker
    72

<message type="tool">     1	"""CoachValidator sibling-repo independent tests (TASK-AB-XREPOEV01 AC-002).
     2	
     3	The Coach must be able to run a declared sibling repo's tests independently
     4	and have the results land in the evidence bundle, with a failed/unrunnable
     5	suite blocking the turn.
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	from pathlib import Path
    11	
    12	import pytest
    13	
    14	from guardkit.orchestrator.coach_verification import HonestyVerification

    20	def _bundle(**kw) -&gt; CoachEvidenceBundle:
    21	    """Build a minimal bundle (only ``honesty`` is required)."""
    22	    return CoachEvidenceBundle(honesty=HonestyVerification(verified=True), **kw)

    25	class TestRunEvidenceRepoTests:
    26	    def test_no_evidence_repos_returns_empty(self, tmp_path):
    27	        validator = CoachValidator(str(tmp_path))
    28	        assert validator.run_evidence_repo_tests() == []
    29	
    30	    def test_runs_declared_passing_suite(self, tmp_path):
    31	        factory = tmp_path / "guardkitfactory"
    32	        factory.mkdir()
    33	        repo = EvidenceRepo(
    34	            name="guardkitfactory",
    35	            root=factory,
    36	            test_command="python -c 'import sys; sys.exit(0)'",
    37	        )
    38	        validator = CoachValidator(str(tmp_path), evidence_repos=[repo])
    39	        results = validator.run_evidence_repo_tests()
    40	        assert len(results) == 1
    41	        assert results[0].repo_name == "guardkitfactory"
    42	        assert results[0].ran is True
    43	        assert results[0].passed is True
    44	
    45	    def test_runs_declared_failing_suite(self, tmp_path):
    46	        factory = tmp_path / "guardkitfactory"
    47	        factory.mkdir()
    48	        repo = EvidenceRepo(
    49	            name="guardkitfactory",
    50	            root=factory,
    51	            test_command="python -c 'import sys; sys.exit(1)'",
    52	        )
    53	        validator = CoachValidator(str(tmp_path), evidence_repos=[repo])
    54	        results = validator.run_evidence_repo_tests()
    55	        assert results[0].ran is True
    56	        assert results[0].passed is False
    57	
    58	    def test_sibling_interpreter_wins_over_worktree_venv(self, tmp_path):
    59	        """TASK-FIX-SIBTESTENV01: sibling tests pin to the sibling's OWN
    60	        interpreter — never to the worktree venv the validator was
    61	        constructed with (the FEAT-10AC run-2 mis-pin)."""
    62	
    63	        def _fake_interpreter(path):
    64	            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    65	            path.write_text('#!/bin/sh\necho "$0" &gt; ran_by.txt\nexit 0\n')
    66	            path.chmod(0o755)
    67	            return path
    68	
    69	        worktree = tmp_path / "worktree"
    70	        worktree.mkdir()
    71	        worktree_python = _fake_interpreter(
    72	            worktree / ".venv" / "bin" / "python"
    73	        )
    74	        sibling = tmp_path / "guardkitfactory"
    75	        sibling.mkdir()
    76	        sibling_python = _fake_interpreter(
    77	            sibling / ".venv" / "bin" / "python"
    78	        )
    79	
    80	        repo = EvidenceRepo(
    81	            name="guardkitfactory",
    82	            root=sibling,
    83	            test_command="pytest -q",
    84	        )
    85	        validator = CoachValidator(
    86	            str(worktree),
    87	            evidence_repos=[repo],
    88	            venv_python=str(worktree_python),
    89	        )
    90	        results = validator.run_evidence_repo_tests()
    91	        assert results[0].ran is True
    92	        assert results[0].passed is True
    93	
    94	        ran_by = (sibling / "ran_by.txt").read_text()
    95	        assert str(sibling_python) in ran_by
    96	        assert str(worktree_python) not in ran_by
